| ko | N_ios | 답장은 한 번만 써 두면 누구에게 보내든 쓸 수 있습니다. 이제 컷링 안에 빈칸을 둘 수 있어서, 받는 사람 이름이나 주문 번호처럼 매번 달라지는 부분은 비워 두면 됩니다. 빈칸이 있는 컷링을 탭하면 Cutling 키보드가 이름을 묻고, 전에 썼던 이름도 보여 주어 한 번만 탭하면 고를 수 있습니다. 그런 다음 완성된 답장을 입력합니다. 이름을 먼저 입력하고 나서 컷링을 탭해도 됩니다. /  / 날짜는 신경 쓸 일이 없습니다. 컷링에 오늘 날짜, 내일 날짜, 일주일 뒤 날짜나 현재 시간을 넣어 두면 쓰는 날에 맞는 값으로 나옵니다. ‘베이직’과 ‘프리미엄’ 같은 요금제 이름 중에서 하나를 고르게 하거나, 마지막으로 복사한 텍스트를 불러오거나, 이어서 입력할 자리에 커서를 놓아 둘 수도 있습니다. 이 기능은 모두 컷링을 편집할 때 ‘변수’ 버튼 안에 있습니다. 직접 입력한 중괄호는 일반 텍스트로 남기 때문에 이미 만들어 둔 컷링은 전과 똑같이 동작합니다. /  / 컷링은 Apple의 메모 앱에서 글을 쓰듯이 쓰면 되고, 새로 배울 것이 없습니다. 컷링의 텍스트가 이제 별도 페이지로 열리고, 여기서 볼드체, 이탤릭체, 밑줄, 취소선, 하이라이트, 고정폭 글꼴, 링크와 구분점 목록, 대시 목록, 번호 목록을 쓸 수 있습니다. /  / 저장 공간도 훨씬 넓어졌습니다. 텍스트 컷링은 100개에서 5,000개로, 이미지는 25개에서 500개로 늘었습니다. 컷링 하나에 10,000자까지 담을 수 있으니 이메일 한 통을 통째로 넣기에 충분합니다. /  / Cutling 키보드의 키는 이제 모두 탭하면 바로 입력됩니다. 서식이 있는 컷링도 다른 컷링과 똑같이 입력되고, 길게 누르면 서식째 복사됩니다. /  / Cutling 키보드 추가를 끝내지 못했다면 Cutling에서 ‘키보드 설정’을 열면 됩니다. 새 가이드가 설정 앱에서 거치는 세 단계를 짧은 영상으로 보여 줍니다. | Write a reply just once and you can use it whoever you send it to. A cutling can now have a blank in it, so the part that differs each time, such as the recipient's name or an order number, can be left empty. When you tap a cutling that has a blank, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name and also shows the names you used before, so one tap picks one. Then it types the finished reply. You can also type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / Dates need no attention from you. Put today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the current time in a cutling and it comes out with the value that fits the day you use it. A cutling can also have you pick one from plan names such as 'Basic' and 'Premium', bring in the text you copied last, or leave the cursor where you will go on typing. All of these are inside the 'Variables' button when you edit a cutling. Curly braces you typed yourself stay ordinary text, so the cutlings you already made behave exactly as before. /  / You write a cutling the way you write in Apple's Notes app, and there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens as a separate page, and there you can use bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, a monospaced font, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is also far more room. Text cutlings went from 100 to 5,000 and images from 25 to 500. One cutling holds up to 10,000 characters, enough to put in a whole email. /  / Every key on the Cutling keyboard now types as soon as you tap it. A cutling with formatting types just like the others, and touching and holding it copies it with its formatting. /  / If you never finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open 'Keyboard Setup' in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps you go through in the Settings app as a short video. |
| ko | N_mac | 답장은 한 번만 써 두면 누구에게 보내든 쓸 수 있습니다. 이제 컷링 안에 빈칸을 둘 수 있어서, 받는 사람 이름이나 주문 번호처럼 매번 달라지는 부분은 비워 두면 됩니다. ⌘⇧V로 그 컷링을 고르면 붙여넣기 전에 짧은 입력 양식이 이름을 묻고, 전에 썼던 이름은 클릭 한 번으로 고를 수 있습니다. /  / 날짜는 신경 쓸 일이 없습니다. 오늘 날짜, 내일 날짜, 일주일 뒤 날짜나 현재 시간을 넣어 두면 컷링을 쓰는 날에 맞는 값으로 나옵니다. ‘베이직’과 ‘프리미엄’ 같은 요금제 이름 중에서 하나를 고르게 하거나, 마지막으로 복사한 텍스트를 불러올 수도 있습니다. 이 기능은 모두 컷링을 편집할 때 텍스트 바로 아래에 있는 ‘변수 추가’ 버튼 안에 있습니다. 직접 입력한 중괄호는 일반 텍스트로 남기 때문에 이미 만들어 둔 컷링을 붙여넣은 결과는 전과 같습니다. /  / 저장 공간도 훨씬 넓어졌습니다. 텍스트 컷링은 100개에서 5,000개로, 이미지는 25개에서 500개로 늘었습니다. 컷링 하나에 10,000자까지 담을 수 있으니 이메일 한 통을 통째로 넣기에 충분합니다. | Write a reply just once and you can use it whoever you send it to. A cutling can now have a blank in it, so the part that differs each time, such as the recipient's name or an order number, can be left empty. When you pick that cutling with ⌘⇧V, a short form asks for the name before it pastes, and the names you used before can be picked with one click. /  / Dates need no attention from you. Put in today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the current time and it comes out with the value that fits the day you use the cutling. A cutling can also have you pick one from plan names such as 'Basic' and 'Premium', or bring in the text you copied last. All of these are inside the 'Add Variable' button right below the text when you edit a cutling. Curly braces you typed yourself stay ordinary text, so what the cutlings you already made paste is the same as before. /  / There is also far more room. Text cutlings went from 100 to 5,000 and images from 25 to 500. One cutling holds up to 10,000 characters, enough to put in a whole email. |
| lt | N_ios | Atsakymą parašai vieną kartą, ir jis tinka visiems, kam jį siunti. Cutlinge dabar gali palikti tuščią vietą tam, kas kaskart skiriasi, pavyzdžiui, vardui ar užsakymo numeriui. Kai bakstelėji tokį cutlingą, Cutling klaviatūra paklausia vardo, parodo anksčiau naudotus vardus, kad reikiamą pasirinktum vienu bakstelėjimu, ir tada įveda visą atsakymą. Gali ir pirma parašyti vardą, o cutlingą bakstelėti po to. /  / Datomis rūpintis nereikia. Į cutlingą gali įdėti šiandienos ar rytojaus datą, datą po savaitės arba laiką, ir jie visada bus teisingi tą dieną, kai cutlingą naudoji. Cutlingas taip pat gali pasiūlyti rinktis iš kelių variantų, pavyzdžiui, iš planų „Bazinis“ ir „Premium“, įterpti paskutinį nukopijuotą tekstą arba palikti žymeklį ten, kur nori rašyti toliau. Visa tai rasi po mygtuku „Kintamieji“, kai redaguoji cutlingą. Riestiniai skliaustai, kuriuos tekste parašei tu, lieka paprastu tekstu, todėl jau turimi cutlingai veikia taip, kaip veikė. /  / Cutlingą dabar rašai taip pat, kaip Apple programėlėje „Užrašai“, ir nieko naujo mokytis nereikia. Cutlingo tekstas atsidaro kaip atskiras puslapis, o jame gali naudoti pusjuodį šriftą, kursyvą, pabraukimą, perbraukimą, paryškinimą, vienodo pločio šriftą, nuorodas ir punktų, brūkšnelių bei numeruotus sąrašus. /  / Vietos dabar kur kas daugiau: telpa 5 000 teksto cutlingų ir 500 vaizdų, o anksčiau tilpo 100 ir 25. Viename cutlinge gali būti iki 10 000 simbolių, tiek užtenka visam el. laiškui. /  / Cutling klaviatūroje dabar kiekvienas klavišas įveda tekstą. Kai bakstelėji formatuotą cutlingą, jis įvedamas kaip bet kuris kitas, o kai jį palieti ir palaikai, nukopijuojamas kartu su formatavimu. /  / Jei Cutling klaviatūros taip ir nebaigei pridėti, atverk Cutling ir bakstelėk „Klaviatūros sąranka“. Naujas vadovas trumpu vaizdo įrašu parodo tris žingsnius programėlėje „Nustatymai“. | You write a reply one time, and it suits everyone you send it to. In a cutling you can now leave an empty place for what differs each time, for example for a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names used earlier so that you pick the right one with one tap, and then types the whole reply. You can also write the name first and tap the cutling after that. /  / There is no need to look after dates. Into a cutling you can put today's or tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the time, and they will always be correct on the day you use the cutling. A cutling can also offer a choice from several options, for example from the plans "Basic" and "Premium", insert the last copied text, or leave the cursor where you want to keep writing. You will find all of this under the button "Variables" when you edit a cutling. Curly braces that you wrote in the text yourself stay ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work the way they worked. /  / You now write a cutling the same way as in Apple's app "Notes", and there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text opens as a separate page, and in it you can use bold type, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, fixed-width type, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is now far more room: 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images fit, and before 100 and 25 fit. One cutling can hold up to 10,000 characters, that much is enough for a whole email. /  / On the Cutling keyboard every key now types text. When you tap a formatted cutling, it is typed like any other, and when you touch and hold it, it is copied together with its formatting. /  / If you never finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open Cutling and tap "Keyboard Setup". The new guide shows the three steps in the app "Settings" as a short video. |
| lt | N_mac | Atsakymą parašai vieną kartą, ir jis tinka visiems, kam jį siunti. Cutlinge dabar gali palikti tuščią vietą tam, kas kaskart skiriasi, pavyzdžiui, vardui ar užsakymo numeriui. Kai paspaudi ⌘⇧V ir pasirenki tokį cutlingą, trumpa forma prieš įklijuojant paklausia vardo, o anksčiau naudoti vardai pasiekiami vienu spustelėjimu. /  / Datomis rūpintis nereikia: šiandienos ar rytojaus data, data po savaitės arba laikas visada bus teisingi tą dieną, kai cutlingą naudoji. Cutlingas taip pat gali pasiūlyti rinktis iš kelių variantų, pavyzdžiui, iš planų „Bazinis“ ir „Premium“, arba įterpti paskutinį nukopijuotą tekstą. | lv | N_ios | Uzraksti atbildi vienreiz, un tā der visiem, kam to sūti. Tagad cutlingā var atstāt tukšumu tai daļai, kas katru reizi mainās, piemēram, saņēmēja vārdam vai pasūtījuma numuram. Kad pieskaries šādam cutlingam, Cutling tastatūra pajautā vārdu, parāda iepriekš lietotos vārdus, lai vienu varētu izvēlēties ar vienu pieskārienu, un tad uzraksta visu atbildi. Vari arī vispirms uzrakstīt vārdu un tikai tad pieskarties cutlingam. /  / Par datumiem tev nav jādomā. Vari ielikt šodienas vai rītdienas datumu, datumu pēc nedēļas vai pulksteņa laiku, un tas būs pareizs dienā, kad cutlingu lietosi. Tāpat cutling var piedāvāt izvēli starp variantiem (piemēram, Pamata vai Premium), ievietot pēdējo nokopēto tekstu vai atstāt kursoru vietā, kur gribi turpināt rakstīt. To visu atradīsi zem pogas „Mainīgie“, kad rediģē cutlingu. Tevis ierakstītās figūriekavas joprojām ir parasts teksts, tāpēc tavi līdzšinējie cutlings darbojas tāpat kā līdz šim. /  / Rakstīt cutlingu vari tāpat, kā raksti Apple lietotnē Notes, un nekas jauns nav jāmācās. Teksts tagad atveras atsevišķā lapā, kur pieejams treknraksts, kursīvs, pasvītrojums, pārsvītrojums, izcelšana, vienplatuma fonts un saites, kā arī aizzīmju, domuzīmju un numurēti saraksti. /  / Vietas ir daudz vairāk. Tagad vari saglabāt 5000 tekstu un 500 attēlu, līdz šim robeža bija 100 un 25. Vienā cutlingā var būt līdz 10 000 rakstzīmju, un ar to pietiek veselai e-pasta vēstulei. /  / Cutling tastatūrā tagad raksta katrs taustiņš. Kad pieskaries formatētam cutlingam, tas tiek ierakstīts tāpat kā jebkurš cits. Ja turi to nospiestu, tas tiek nokopēts kopā ar formatējumu. /  / Ja Cutling tastatūras pievienošanu tā arī nepabeidzi, atver lietotnē Cutling sadaļu „Tastatūras iestatīšana“. Jaunais ceļvedis īsā video parāda trīs soļus lietotnē Iestatījumi. | Write a reply once, and it suits everyone you send it to. Now in a cutling you can leave a gap for the part that changes each time, for example for the recipient's name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names used before, so that one could be picked with one tap, and then types the whole reply. You can also first type the name and only then tap the cutling. /  / About dates you do not have to think. You can put in today's or tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the clock time, and it will be right on the day you use the cutling. Likewise a cutling can offer a choice between options (for example Pamata or Premium), put in the last copied text or leave the cursor at the place where you want to keep typing. All that you will find under the button "Mainīgie" when you edit a cutling. Curly braces typed by you are still ordinary text, so your cutlings so far work the same as until now. /  / You can write a cutling the same way you write in Apple's app Notes, and nothing new has to be learnt. The text now opens on a separate page, where bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospaced font and links are available, as well as bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is much more room. Now you can save 5000 texts and 500 images, until now the limit was 100 and 25. One cutling can have up to 10,000 characters, and that is enough for a whole email letter. /  / On the Cutling keyboard every key now types. When you tap a formatted cutling, it is typed in the same as any other. If you hold it down, it is copied together with the formatting. /  / If you never did finish adding the Cutling keyboard, open the section "Tastatūras iestatīšana" in the Cutling app. The new guide shows in a short video the three steps in the app Iestatījumi (Settings). |

ഉപയോഗിക്കുന്ന ദിവസം അത് കൃത്യമായി വരും. കട്ട്‌ലിംഗിൽ കുറച്ച് തിരഞ്ഞെടുപ്പുകൾ ചേർത്ത് അതിലൊന്ന് എടുക്കാം (ഉദാഹരണത്തിന് ബേസിക് അല്ലെങ്കിൽ പ്രീമിയം പ്ലാൻ), അവസാനം പകർത്തിയ ടെക്സ്റ്റ് അതിലേക്ക് കൊണ്ടുവരാം, അല്ലെങ്കിൽ നിങ്ങൾ തുടർന്ന് ടൈപ്പ് ചെയ്യാൻ ആഗ്രഹിക്കുന്നിടത്ത് കഴ്സർ നിർത്താം. കട്ട്‌ലിംഗ് എഡിറ്റ് ചെയ്യുമ്പോൾ ഇവയെല്ലാം “വേരിയബിളുകൾ” എന്ന ബട്ടണിലുണ്ട്. നിങ്ങൾ തന്നെ ടൈപ്പ് ചെയ്ത ചുരുണ്ട ബ്രേസുകൾ സാധാരണ ടെക്സ്റ്റായിത്തന്നെ തുടരും, അതുകൊണ്ട് നിങ്ങളുടെ പക്കൽ ഇപ്പോഴുള്ള കട്ട്‌ലിംഗുകൾ പഴയപടി പ്രവർത്തിക്കും. /  / Apple-ന്റെ കുറിപ്പുകൾ ആപ്പിൽ എഴുതുന്നതുപോലെ തന്നെ കട്ട്‌ലിംഗും എഴുതാം, പുതുതായി ഒന്നും പഠിക്കേണ്ട. കട്ട്‌ലിംഗിന്റെ ടെക്സ്റ്റ് ഇനി സ്വന്തമായ ഒരു പേജിലാണ് തുറക്കുക. അവിടെ ബോൾഡ്, ഇറ്റാലിക്, അടിവര, സ്ട്രൈക്ക്ത്രൂ, ഹൈലൈറ്റ്, മോണോസ്പേസ്, ലിങ്കുകൾ, ബുള്ളറ്റ്, ഡാഷ്, അക്കമിട്ട ലിസ്റ്റുകൾ എന്നിവയുണ്ട്. /  / സ്ഥലവും ഏറെ കൂടി. മുമ്പ് 100 വാചക കട്ട്‌ലിംഗുകളും 25 ചിത്രങ്ങളും മാത്രമേ സേവ് ചെയ്യാൻ കഴിഞ്ഞിരുന്നുള്ളൂ. ഇനി 5,000 വാചക കട്ട്‌ലിംഗുകളും 500 ചിത്രങ്ങളും സേവ് ചെയ്യാം. ഒരു കട്ട്‌ലിംഗിൽ 10,000 അക്ഷരങ്ങൾ വരെ എഴുതാം, ഒരു മുഴുവൻ ഇമെയിലിന് അത് മതി. /  / Cutling കീബോർഡിലെ എല്ലാ കീയും ഇനി ടൈപ്പ് ചെയ്യും. ഫോർമാറ്റ് ചെയ്ത കട്ട്‌ലിംഗ് ടാപ്പ് ചെയ്താൽ മറ്റുള്ളവ പോലെ തന്നെ ടൈപ്പ് ആകും. അത് അമർത്തിപ്പിടിച്ചാൽ ഫോർമാറ്റിങ്ങോടെ പകർത്താം. /  / Cutling കീബോർഡ് ചേർക്കുന്നത് ഇതുവരെ പൂർത്തിയാക്കിയിട്ടില്ലെങ്കിൽ, Cutling-ൽ “കീബോർഡ് സജ്ജീകരണം” തുറക്കുക. ക്രമീകരണം ആപ്പിൽ ചെയ്യേണ്ട മൂന്ന് ഘട്ടങ്ങൾ പുതിയ വഴികാട്ടി ഒരു ചെറിയ വീഡിയോയായി കാണിക്കും. | Writing a reply once is enough; whoever you send it to, you can use that same one. For the part that changes each time, for example a customer's name or an order number, you can now leave a blank in a cutling. When you tap that cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name. It also shows the names you used before, and you can pick one of them with a single tap. Then it types the whole reply itself. If you want it quicker still, it is enough to type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For the date you need do nothing. In a cutling you can add today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use it, it comes out exactly right. In a cutling you can add a few choices and take one of them (for example the Basic or Premium plan), bring the text you copied last into it, or stop the cursor where you want to go on typing. While editing a cutling, all of these are in the button called “Variables”. Curly braces you typed yourself stay as ordinary text, so the cutlings you have now work the old way. /  / You can write a cutling just the way you write in Apple's Notes app; there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens on a page of its own. There you have bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / Room has grown a great deal too. Before, only 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be saved. Now you can save 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images. In one cutling you can write up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email. /  / Every key on the Cutling keyboard now types. If you tap a formatted cutling it gets typed just like the others. If you touch and hold it, you can copy it with its formatting. /  / If you have not yet finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open “Keyboard Setup” in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps to do in the Settings app as a short video. |

In a cutling you can add a few choices and take one of them (for example the Basic or Premium plan), or bring the text you copied last into it. While editing a cutling, all of these are in the button called “Add Variable” just below the text. Curly braces you typed yourself stay as ordinary text, so the cutlings you have now paste the old way. /  / Room has grown a great deal too. Before, only 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be saved. Now you can save 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images. In one cutling you can write up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email. |

| mr-IN | N_ios | उत्तर एकदाच लिहा, ते तुम्ही ज्यांना पाठवता त्या प्रत्येकासाठी उपयोगी पडते. कटलिंगमध्ये आता बदलणाऱ्या भागासाठी रिकामी जागा ठेवता येते, उदाहरणार्थ नाव किंवा ऑर्डर क्रमांक. असे कटलिंग टॅप केले की Cutling कीबोर्ड नाव विचारतो, तुम्ही आधी वापरलेली नावे दाखवतो म्हणजे एका टॅपमध्ये त्यातले एक निवडता येते, आणि मग पूर्ण उत्तर लिहितो. नाव आधी टाइप करून मग कटलिंग टॅप केले तरी चालते. /  / तारखांसाठी तुम्हाला काहीच करावे लागत नाही. कटलिंगमध्ये आजची तारीख, उद्याची तारीख, एका आठवड्यानंतरची तारीख किंवा वेळ ठेवता येते, आणि ज्या दिवशी कटलिंग वापराल त्या दिवशी ती बरोबर येते. कटलिंगमध्ये काही निवडींमधून एक निवडायची सोयही ठेवता येते (उदा. बेसिक किंवा प्रीमियम प्लॅन), तुम्ही शेवटी कॉपी केलेला मजकूर आणता येतो, किंवा जिथून पुढे लिहायचे आहे तिथे कर्सर ठेवता येतो. कटलिंग संपादित करताना ‘व्हेरिएबल’ बटणावर टॅप केल्यावर हे सगळे मिळते. तुम्ही स्वतः टाइप केलेले कर्ली ब्रेस { } साधा मजकूरच राहतात, त्यामुळे तुमचे आधीचे कटलिंग पूर्वीसारखेच चालतात. /  / कटलिंग आता तुम्ही Apple च्या Notes ॲपमध्ये लिहिता तसेच लिहिता येते, नवीन काही शिकावे लागत नाही. कटलिंगचा मजकूर आता स्वतःच्या वेगळ्या पानावर उघडतो, आणि तिथे बोल्ड, इटॅलिक, अधोरेखित, स्ट्राइकथ्रू, हायलाइट, मोनोस्पेस, लिंक, तसेच बुलेट असलेली, डॅश असलेली आणि क्रमांकित यादी वापरता येते. /  / जागा आता खूप जास्त आहे. पूर्वी 100 मजकूर कटलिंग आणि 25 प्रतिमा ठेवता येत होत्या, आता 5,000 मजकूर कटलिंग आणि 500 प्रतिमा ठेवता येतात. एका कटलिंगमध्ये 10,000 अक्षरांपर्यंत मजकूर मावतो, तेवढ्यात एक अख्खा ईमेल बसतो. /  / Cutling कीबोर्डवरची प्रत्येक की आता लिहिते. फॉरमॅट केलेले कटलिंग टॅप केले की ते इतर कोणत्याही कटलिंगसारखेच लिहिले जाते. ते दाबून ठेवले की फॉरमॅटिंगसह कॉपी होते. /  / Cutling कीबोर्ड जोडण्याचे काम अर्धवट राहिले असेल, तर Cutling मध्ये ‘कीबोर्ड सेटअप’ उघडा. नवीन मार्गदर्शक सेटिंग्ज ॲपमधील तीन पायऱ्या एका छोट्या व्हिडिओमध्ये दाखवतो. | Write the reply just once; it comes in useful for each person you send it to. A cutling can now keep a blank space for the part that changes, for example a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names you used before so that one of them can be picked in one tap, and then writes the full reply. It also works if you type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For dates you have to do nothing at all. A cutling can keep today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use the cutling it comes out right. A cutling can also keep a way to pick one out of a few choices (e.g. the Basic or the Premium plan), can bring in the text you copied last, or can keep the cursor at the place you want to go on writing from. While editing a cutling, all of this is found on tapping the ‘Variables’ button. Curly braces { } you typed yourself stay plain text, so your earlier cutlings work just as before. /  / A cutling can now be written just the way you write in Apple's Notes app; nothing new has to be learned. A cutling's text now opens on a separate page of its own, and there you can use bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, link, as well as bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is now far more room. Before, 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be kept; now 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images can be kept. One cutling holds text of up to 10,000 characters; a whole email fits in that much. /  / Every key on the Cutling keyboard now writes. When you tap a formatted cutling, it is written just like any other cutling. When you press and hold it, it is copied with its formatting. /  / If the job of adding the Cutling keyboard was left half done, open ‘Keyboard Setup’ in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app in one short video. |

| ms | N_ios | Balasan yang anda tulis sekali boleh dihantar kepada sesiapa sahaja. Sesebuah cutling kini boleh mempunyai tempat kosong untuk bahagian yang berubah, seperti nama atau nombor pesanan. Apabila anda mengetik cutling itu, papan kekunci Cutling bertanya nama, menunjukkan nama yang pernah anda gunakan supaya boleh dipilih dengan satu ketikan, lalu menaip balasan yang lengkap. Anda juga boleh menaip nama dahulu, kemudian mengetik cutling itu. /  / Tarikh pula tidak perlu anda isi sendiri. Cutling boleh mengandungi tarikh hari ini, esok, seminggu dari sekarang atau masa, dan semuanya tepat pada hari cutling itu digunakan. Cutling juga boleh memberi pilihan (contohnya pelan Asas atau Premium), memasukkan teks terakhir yang anda salin, atau meninggalkan kursor di tempat anda mahu sambung menaip. Semua ini ada pada butang “Pemboleh Ubah” semasa anda mengedit cutling. Pendakap kerinting yang anda taip sendiri kekal sebagai teks biasa, jadi cutling yang sudah anda simpan berfungsi seperti dahulu. /  / Anda boleh menulis cutling seperti menulis dalam app Nota Apple, tanpa perlu belajar apa-apa yang baharu. Teks cutling kini dibuka sebagai halaman tersendiri dengan format tebal, italik, garis bawah, garis lorek, serlahan, fon lebar tetap dan pautan, serta senarai berbulet, bersengkang dan bernombor. /  / Ruang juga jauh lebih besar. Anda boleh menyimpan 5,000 cutling teks dan 500 imej, berbanding 100 dan 25 sebelum ini. Satu cutling boleh memuatkan sehingga 10,000 aksara, cukup untuk satu emel penuh. /  / Setiap kekunci pada papan kekunci Cutling kini menaip. Ketik cutling berformat dan ia ditaip seperti cutling lain. Sentuh dan tahan cutling itu untuk menyalinnya bersama formatnya. /  / Jika anda belum selesai menambah papan kekunci Cutling, buka “Persediaan papan kekunci” dalam Cutling. Kies je zo’n cutling met ⌘⇧V, dan vraagt een kort formulier om de naam voordat hij wordt geplakt, en de namen die je eerder gebruikte, staan één klik verder. /  / Voor datums hoef je niets te doen. Een cutling kan de datum van vandaag, van morgen of van over een week bevatten, of de tijd, en die klopt op de dag dat je hem gebruikt. Hij kan je ook laten kiezen uit een paar opties, bijvoorbeeld Basis of Premium, of de tekst invoegen die je het laatst hebt gekopieerd. Dat vind je allemaal onder de knop ‘Voeg variabele toe’, die direct onder de tekst staat als je een cutling bewerkt. Accolades die je zelf hebt getypt, blijven gewone tekst, dus de cutlings die je al had, worden geplakt zoals je gewend bent. /  / Er is veel meer ruimte. Je bewaart nu 5.000 tekst-cutlings en 500 afbeeldingen, waar dat eerst 100 en 25 was. Eén cutling mag 10.000 tekens lang zijn, genoeg voor een hele e-mail. | You write a reply one time and use it for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now have a fill-in field for the bit that is different every time, such as a name or an order number. When you pick such a cutling with ⌘⇧V, a short form asks for the name before it is pasted, and the names you used earlier are one click further. /  / For dates you don't have to do anything. A cutling can hold the date of today, of tomorrow or of a week from now, or the time, and it is right on the day you use it. It can also let you choose from a few options, for example Basis or Premium, or insert the text you copied last. You find all of that under the 'Voeg variabele toe' button, which is right below the text when you edit a cutling. Curly braces you typed yourself stay ordinary text, so the cutlings you already had are pasted the way you are used to. /  / There is much more room. You now keep 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images, where that was 100 and 25 before. One cutling may be 10,000 characters long, enough for a whole email. |

| no | N_ios | Skriv et svar én gang, så passer det til alle du sender det til. En cutling kan nå ha et felt for det som endrer seg, for eksempel et navn eller et ordrenummer. Når du trykker på cutlingen, spør Cutling-tastaturet om navnet, viser navnene du har brukt før, så du kan velge ett med ett trykk, og skriver så hele svaret. Du kan også skrive navnet først og trykke på cutlingen etterpå. /  / Datoer trenger du ikke å gjøre noe med. En cutling kan inneholde dagens dato, morgendagens, datoen om en uke eller klokkeslettet, og det blir riktig den dagen du bruker den. Den kan også la deg velge mellom alternativer (for eksempel Basis eller Premium), hente inn teksten du kopierte sist, eller la markøren stå der du vil skrive videre. Alt dette finner du under knappen «Variabler» når du redigerer en cutling. Krøllparenteser du har skrevet selv, er fortsatt vanlig tekst, så cutlingene du allerede har, fungerer som før. /  / Du kan skrive en cutling slik du skriver i Notater-appen fra Apple, uten å lære noe nytt. Teksten åpnes nå på en egen side med fet, kursiv, understreking, gjennomstreking, utheving, monospace, lenker og punktlister, streklister og nummererte lister. /  / Du har fått mye mer plass. Nå kan du ha 5000 tekst-cutlings og 500 bilder, mot 100 og 25 før. Én cutling kan være på opptil 10 000 tegn, og det er nok til en hel e-post. /  / Alle tastene på Cutling-tastaturet skriver nå. Trykker du på en cutling med formatering, skrives den inn som alle andre. Holder du den inne, kopieres den med formateringen i behold. /  / Ble du aldri ferdig med å legge til Cutling-tastaturet, åpner du «Tastaturoppsett» i Cutling. Den nye veiledningen viser de tre trinnene i Innstillinger-appen som en kort video. | Write a reply one time, then it fits everyone you send it to. A cutling can now have a field for what changes, for example a name or an order number. When you tap the cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names you have used before, so you can pick one with one tap, and then types the whole reply. You can also type the name first and tap the cutling afterwards. /  / Dates you do not need to do anything with. A cutling can contain today's date, tomorrow's, the date in a week or the time of day, and it comes out right on the day you use it. It can also let you choose between options (for example Basis or Premium), bring in the text you copied last, or leave the cursor where you want to keep writing. All this you find under the button "Variabler" when you edit a cutling. Curly braces you have typed yourself are still ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as before. /  / You can write a cutling the way you write in the Notes app from Apple, without learning anything new. The text now opens on a page of its own with bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links and bulleted lists, dashed lists and numbered lists. /  / You have got much more room. Now you can have 5000 text cutlings and 500 images, against 100 and 25 before. One cutling can be up to 10,000 characters, and that is enough for a whole email. /  / All the keys on the Cutling keyboard now type. If you tap a cutling with formatting, it is typed in like all the others. If you hold it down, it is copied with the formatting kept. /  / If you never finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open "Tastaturoppsett" in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app as a short video. |

| or-IN | E3 | Cutling ଆଗ ନାମ ପଚାରେ, ଆଜିର ତାରିଖ ନିଜେ ଭରେ, ପରେ ବାକିଟା ଟାଇପ୍ କରେ। ଆପଣ ଉତ୍ତର ଥରେ ଲେଖନ୍ତି, ଆଉ ହାତରେ ସୁଧାରିବା ଦରକାର ନାହିଁ। | Cutling first asks the name, fills in today's date by itself, then types the rest. You write the reply once, and there is no more need to correct it by hand. |
| or-IN | E3s | Cutling ଆଗ ନାମ ପଚାରେ, ଆଜିର ତାରିଖ ନିଜେ ଭରେ, ପରେ ବାକିଟା ଟାଇପ୍ କରେ। ଥରେ ଲେଖିଲେ ହେଲା। Cutling ରେ ବ୍ଲାକ୍ ଫ୍ରାଇଡେ ସେଲ୍ ଚାଲିଛି। | Cutling first asks the name, fills in today's date by itself, then types the rest. Write it once and that's it. A Black Friday sale is running on Cutling. |

| or-IN | N_ios | ଉତ୍ତର ଥରେ ଲେଖିଲେ, ଆପଣ ଯେତେ ଲୋକଙ୍କୁ ପଠାନ୍ତି ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ ଚଳିବ। ଏବେ ଗୋଟିଏ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂରେ ବଦଳୁଥିବା ଅଂଶ ପାଇଁ ଖାଲି ସ୍ଥାନ ରଖିହେବ, ଯେମିତି ନାମ କିମ୍ବା ଅର୍ଡର ନମ୍ବର। ଆପଣ ସେହି କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଟ୍ୟାପ୍ କଲେ Cutling କୀବୋର୍ଡ ନାମ ପଚାରେ, ଆଗରୁ ବ୍ୟବହାର କରିଥିବା ନାମଗୁଡ଼ିକ ଦେଖାଏ ଯେମିତି ଗୋଟିଏ ଟ୍ୟାପ୍‌ରେ ବାଛିହେବ, ତାପରେ ପୂରା ଉତ୍ତର ଟାଇପ୍ କରିଦିଏ। ଚାହିଁଲେ ଆଗ ନାମ ଟାଇପ୍ କରି ତାପରେ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଟ୍ୟାପ୍ କରିପାରିବେ। /  / ତାରିଖ ପାଇଁ ଆପଣଙ୍କୁ କିଛି କରିବାକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ନାହିଁ। ଗୋଟିଏ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂରେ ଆଜିର ତାରିଖ, ଆସନ୍ତାକାଲିର ତାରିଖ, ଏକ ସପ୍ତାହ ପରର ତାରିଖ କିମ୍ବା ସମୟ ରଖିହେବ, ଆଉ ଯେଉଁଦିନ ବ୍ୟବହାର କରିବେ ସେଦିନ ତାହା ଠିକ୍ ଆସିବ। ଗୋଟିଏ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଆପଣଙ୍କୁ କିଛି ବିକଳ୍ପ ମଧ୍ୟରୁ ବାଛିବାକୁ ବି ଦେଇପାରେ (ଯେମିତି ମାସିକ ପ୍ଲାନ କିମ୍ବା ବାର୍ଷିକ ପ୍ଲାନ), ଆପଣ ଶେଷଥର କପି କରିଥିବା ଟେକ୍ସଟ ଆଣିପାରେ, କିମ୍ବା ଆପଣ ଯେଉଁଠି ଆଗକୁ ଟାଇପ୍ କରିବାକୁ ଚାହାନ୍ତି ସେଠି କର୍ସର ଛାଡ଼ିପାରେ। କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ସମ୍ପାଦନା କଲାବେଳେ ଏ ସବୁ “ଭେରିଏବଲ୍” ବଟନ ତଳେ ମିଳିବ। ଆପଣ ନିଜେ ଟାଇପ୍ କରିଥିବା କର୍ଲି ବ୍ରେସ ସାଧାରଣ ଟେକ୍ସଟ ହୋଇ ରହେ, ତେଣୁ ଆପଣଙ୍କ ପାଖରେ ଆଗରୁ ଥିବା କଟ୍‌ଲିଂଗୁଡ଼ିକ ଆଗ ପରି କାମ କରିବ। /  / ଆପଣ Apple ର Notes ଆପରେ ଯେମିତି ଲେଖନ୍ତି, ସେମିତି କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଲେଖିପାରିବେ, ନୂଆ କିଛି ଶିଖିବାକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ନାହିଁ। କଟ୍‌ଲିଂର ଟେକ୍ସଟ ଏବେ ନିଜର ଅଲଗା ପୃଷ୍ଠାରେ ଖୋଲେ, ଆଉ ସେଥିରେ ଏହି ଫର୍ମାଟ୍ ଅଛି: ବୋଲ୍ଡ୍, ଇଟାଲିକ୍, ଅଣ୍ଡରଲାଇନ୍, ଷ୍ଟ୍ରାଇକଥ୍ରୁ, ହାଇଲାଇଟ୍, ମୋନୋସ୍ପେସ୍, ଲିଙ୍କ୍, ଏବଂ ବୁଲେଟ୍, ଡ୍ୟାସ୍ ଓ କ୍ରମାଙ୍କ ସହ ତାଲିକା। /  / ଜାଗା ବହୁତ ବଢ଼ିଛି। ଏବେ ୫,୦୦୦ଟି ଟେକ୍ସଟ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଓ ୫୦୦ଟି ଛବି ରଖିହେବ, ଆଗରୁ ଏହା ୧୦୦ ଓ ୨୫ ଥିଲା। ଗୋଟିଏ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ୧୦,୦୦୦ ଅକ୍ଷର ଯାଏ ହୋଇପାରେ, ଯାହା ଗୋଟିଏ ପୂରା ଇମେଲ ପାଇଁ ଯଥେଷ୍ଟ। /  / Cutling କୀବୋର୍ଡର ପ୍ରତ୍ୟେକ କୀ ଏବେ ଟାଇପ୍ କରେ। ଫର୍ମାଟ୍ ହୋଇଥିବା କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ଟ୍ୟାପ୍ କଲେ ତାହା ଅନ୍ୟ କଟ୍‌ଲିଂ ପରି ଟାଇପ୍ ହୁଏ। ଦବାଇ ଧରିଲେ ତାହା ଫର୍ମାଟ୍ ସହିତ କପି ହୁଏ। /  / ଯଦି ଆପଣ Cutling କୀବୋର୍ଡ ଯୋଡ଼ିବା ଶେଷ କରିନାହାନ୍ତି, Cutling ରେ “କୀବୋର୍ଡ ସେଟଅପ୍” ଖୋଲନ୍ତୁ। ନୂଆ ଗାଇଡ୍ ସେଟିଂସ ଆପର ତିନୋଟି ପଦକ୍ଷେପକୁ ଗୋଟିଏ ଛୋଟ ଭିଡିଓରେ ଦେଖାଏ। | Write a reply once and it will do for everyone you send it to, however many. Now a cutling can keep a blank for the part that changes, such as a name or an order number. When you tap that cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names you used before so that one can be picked with a single tap, then types out the whole reply. If you like, you can type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For dates you will not have to do anything. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use it, it will come out right. A cutling can also let you choose from a few options (such as a monthly plan or a yearly plan), bring in the text you copied last, or leave the cursor where you want to go on typing. While editing a cutling, all of these are found under the “ଭେରିଏବଲ୍” button. Curly braces you typed yourself stay ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have will work as before. /  / You can write a cutling the way you write in Apple's Notes app, with nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens on a separate page of its own, and it has these formats: bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / The room has grown a lot. You can now keep 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images; before it was 100 and 25. One cutling can be up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for one whole email. /  / Every key on the Cutling keyboard now types. Tap a formatted cutling and it is typed like any other cutling. Press and hold it, and it is copied with its formatting. /  / If you have not finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open “କୀବୋର୍ଡ ସେଟଅପ୍” (Keyboard Setup) in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps of the Settings app in one short video. |

Now a cutling can keep a blank for the part that changes, such as a name or an order number. | pa-IN | N_ios | ਜਵਾਬ ਇੱਕੋ ਵਾਰ ਲਿਖੋ, ਇਹ ਉਨ੍ਹਾਂ ਸਾਰਿਆਂ ਲਈ ਕੰਮ ਆਉਂਦਾ ਹੈ ਜਿਨ੍ਹਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਤੁਸੀਂ ਭੇਜਦੇ ਹੋ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ ਹੁਣ ਬਦਲਣ ਵਾਲੇ ਹਿੱਸੇ ਲਈ ਖਾਲੀ ਥਾਂ ਰੱਖੀ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀ ਹੈ, ਜਿਵੇਂ ਨਾਮ ਜਾਂ ਆਰਡਰ ਨੰਬਰ। ਅਜਿਹੇ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ 'ਤੇ ਟੈਪ ਕਰੋ ਤਾਂ Cutling ਕੀਬੋਰਡ ਨਾਮ ਪੁੱਛਦਾ ਹੈ, ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਰਤੇ ਨਾਮ ਦਿਖਾਉਂਦਾ ਹੈ ਤਾਂ ਜੋ ਇੱਕ ਟੈਪ ਨਾਲ ਉਨ੍ਹਾਂ ਵਿੱਚੋਂ ਇੱਕ ਚੁਣ ਸਕੋ, ਅਤੇ ਫਿਰ ਪੂਰਾ ਜਵਾਬ ਲਿਖ ਦਿੰਦਾ ਹੈ। ਤੁਸੀਂ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਨਾਮ ਟਾਈਪ ਕਰ ਕੇ ਫਿਰ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ 'ਤੇ ਟੈਪ ਵੀ ਕਰ ਸਕਦੇ ਹੋ। /  / ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ਾਂ ਲਈ ਤੁਹਾਨੂੰ ਕੁਝ ਨਹੀਂ ਕਰਨਾ ਪੈਂਦਾ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ ਅੱਜ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼, ਆਉਣ ਵਾਲੇ ਕੱਲ੍ਹ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼, ਹਫ਼ਤੇ ਬਾਅਦ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ ਜਾਂ ਸਮਾਂ ਰੱਖਿਆ ਜਾ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ, ਅਤੇ ਜਿਸ ਦਿਨ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਰਤੋ, ਉਸ ਦਿਨ ਮੁਤਾਬਕ ਸਹੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ ਜਾਂ ਸਮਾਂ ਆਉਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਕੁਝ ਵਿਕਲਪਾਂ ਵਿੱਚੋਂ ਚੋਣ ਵੀ ਕਰਵਾ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ (ਜਿਵੇਂ ਬੇਸਿਕ ਜਾਂ ਪ੍ਰੀਮੀਅਮ ਪਲਾਨ), ਤੁਹਾਡਾ ਆਖ਼ਰੀ ਕਾਪੀ ਕੀਤਾ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਲਿਆ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ, ਜਾਂ ਕਰਸਰ ਉੱਥੇ ਛੱਡ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ ਜਿੱਥੋਂ ਤੁਸੀਂ ਅੱਗੇ ਲਿਖਣਾ ਚਾਹੁੰਦੇ ਹੋ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਸੋਧਣ ਵੇਲੇ ‘ਵੇਰੀਏਬਲ’ ਬਟਨ 'ਤੇ ਟੈਪ ਕਰੋ ਤਾਂ ਇਹ ਸਭ ਮਿਲ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਆਪ ਟਾਈਪ ਕੀਤੇ ਕਰਲੀ ਬਰੇਸ { } ਸਾਦਾ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਹੀ ਰਹਿੰਦੇ ਹਨ, ਇਸ ਲਈ ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਾਲੇ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਾਂਗ ਹੀ ਚੱਲਦੇ ਹਨ। /  / ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਹੁਣ ਉਸੇ ਤਰ੍ਹਾਂ ਲਿਖਿਆ ਜਾ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ ਜਿਵੇਂ ਤੁਸੀਂ Apple ਦੀ Notes ਐਪ ਵਿੱਚ ਲਿਖਦੇ ਹੋ, ਕੁਝ ਨਵਾਂ ਸਿੱਖਣ ਦੀ ਲੋੜ ਨਹੀਂ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਦਾ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਹੁਣ ਆਪਣੇ ਵੱਖਰੇ ਪੰਨੇ 'ਤੇ ਖੁੱਲ੍ਹਦਾ ਹੈ, ਅਤੇ ਉੱਥੇ ਗੂੜ੍ਹਾ, ਇਟੈਲਿਕ, ਰੇਖਾਂਕਿਤ, ਕੱਟਿਆ ਹੋਇਆ, ਹਾਈਲਾਈਟ, ਮੋਨੋਸਪੇਸ, ਲਿੰਕ, ਨਾਲੇ ਬਿੰਦੀਆਂ ਵਾਲੀ, ਡੈਸ਼ ਵਾਲੀ ਤੇ ਨੰਬਰ ਵਾਲੀ ਸੂਚੀ ਵਰਤੀ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀ ਹੈ। /  / ਥਾਂ ਹੁਣ ਬਹੁਤ ਜ਼ਿਆਦਾ ਹੈ। ਪਹਿਲਾਂ 100 ਟੈਕਸਟ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਅਤੇ 25 ਤਸਵੀਰਾਂ ਰੱਖੀਆਂ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀਆਂ ਸਨ, ਹੁਣ 5,000 ਟੈਕਸਟ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਅਤੇ 500 ਤਸਵੀਰਾਂ ਰੱਖੀਆਂ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀਆਂ ਹਨ। ਇੱਕ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ 10,000 ਅੱਖਰ ਤੱਕ ਆ ਜਾਂਦੇ ਹਨ, ਇੰਨੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਪੂਰੀ ਈਮੇਲ ਸਮਾ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ। /  / Cutling ਕੀਬੋਰਡ ਦੀ ਹਰ ਕੁੰਜੀ ਹੁਣ ਲਿਖਦੀ ਹੈ। ਫ਼ਾਰਮੈਟ ਕੀਤੇ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ 'ਤੇ ਟੈਪ ਕਰੋ ਤਾਂ ਉਹ ਬਾਕੀ ਕਟਲਿੰਗਾਂ ਵਾਂਗ ਹੀ ਲਿਖਿਆ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਉਸ ਨੂੰ ਦਬਾ ਕੇ ਰੱਖੋ ਤਾਂ ਉਹ ਫ਼ਾਰਮੈਟ ਸਮੇਤ ਕਾਪੀ ਹੋ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ। /  / ਜੇ Cutling ਕੀਬੋਰਡ ਸ਼ਾਮਲ ਕਰਨਾ ਅਧੂਰਾ ਰਹਿ ਗਿਆ ਸੀ, ਤਾਂ Cutling ਵਿੱਚ ‘ਕੀਬੋਰਡ ਸੈਟਅੱਪ’ ਖੋਲ੍ਹੋ। ਨਵੀਂ ਗਾਈਡ ਸੈਟਿੰਗਾਂ ਐਪ ਦੇ ਤਿੰਨ ਕਦਮ ਇੱਕ ਛੋਟੀ ਵੀਡੀਓ ਵਿੱਚ ਦਿਖਾਉਂਦੀ ਹੈ। | Write the reply just once; it comes in useful for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now keep a blank space for the part that changes, such as a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names you used before so that you can pick one of them with one tap, and then writes out the full reply. You can also type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For dates you don't have to do anything. A cutling can keep today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and the right date or time comes out for the day you use the cutling. A cutling can also have you choose from a few options (such as the Basic or the Premium plan), can bring in the text you copied last, or can leave the cursor at the place you want to go on writing from. While editing a cutling, tap the ‘Variables’ button and all of this is there. Curly braces { } you typed yourself stay plain text, so your earlier cutlings work just as before. /  / A cutling can now be written the same way you write in Apple's Notes app; no need to learn anything new. A cutling's text now opens on a separate page of its own, and there you can use bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, link, along with bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is now much more room. Before, 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be kept; now 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images can be kept. One cutling takes up to 10,000 characters; a whole email fits in that much. /  / Every key of the Cutling keyboard now writes. Tap a formatted cutling and it is written just like the other cutlings. Press and hold it and it is copied along with its format. /  / If adding the Cutling keyboard was left unfinished, open ‘Keyboard Setup’ in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps of the Settings app in one short video. |
| pa-IN | N_mac | ਜਵਾਬ ਇੱਕੋ ਵਾਰ ਲਿਖੋ, ਇਹ ਉਨ੍ਹਾਂ ਸਾਰਿਆਂ ਲਈ ਕੰਮ ਆਉਂਦਾ ਹੈ ਜਿਨ੍ਹਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਤੁਸੀਂ ਭੇਜਦੇ ਹੋ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ ਹੁਣ ਬਦਲਣ ਵਾਲੇ ਹਿੱਸੇ ਲਈ ਖਾਲੀ ਥਾਂ ਰੱਖੀ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀ ਹੈ, ਜਿਵੇਂ ਨਾਮ ਜਾਂ ਆਰਡਰ ਨੰਬਰ। ਅਜਿਹਾ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ⌘⇧V ਨਾਲ ਚੁਣੋ ਤਾਂ ਪੇਸਟ ਕਰਨ ਤੋਂ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਇੱਕ ਛੋਟਾ ਫਾਰਮ ਨਾਮ ਪੁੱਛਦਾ ਹੈ, ਅਤੇ ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਰਤੇ ਨਾਮ ਇੱਕ ਕਲਿੱਕ ਵਿੱਚ ਮਿਲ ਜਾਂਦੇ ਹਨ। /  / ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ਾਂ ਲਈ ਤੁਹਾਨੂੰ ਕੁਝ ਨਹੀਂ ਕਰਨਾ ਪੈਂਦਾ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ ਅੱਜ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼, ਆਉਣ ਵਾਲੇ ਕੱਲ੍ਹ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼, ਹਫ਼ਤੇ ਬਾਅਦ ਦੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ ਜਾਂ ਸਮਾਂ ਰੱਖਿਆ ਜਾ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ, ਅਤੇ ਜਿਸ ਦਿਨ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਰਤੋ, ਉਸ ਦਿਨ ਮੁਤਾਬਕ ਸਹੀ ਤਾਰੀਖ਼ ਜਾਂ ਸਮਾਂ ਆਉਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਕੁਝ ਵਿਕਲਪਾਂ ਵਿੱਚੋਂ ਚੋਣ ਵੀ ਕਰਵਾ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ (ਜਿਵੇਂ ਬੇਸਿਕ ਜਾਂ ਪ੍ਰੀਮੀਅਮ ਪਲਾਨ), ਜਾਂ ਤੁਹਾਡਾ ਆਖ਼ਰੀ ਕਾਪੀ ਕੀਤਾ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਲਿਆ ਸਕਦਾ ਹੈ। ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਸੋਧਣ ਵੇਲੇ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਦੇ ਬਿਲਕੁਲ ਹੇਠਾਂ ‘ਵੇਰੀਏਬਲ ਜੋੜੋ’ ਬਟਨ ਹੈ, ਉਸ 'ਤੇ ਕਲਿੱਕ ਕਰੋ ਤਾਂ ਇਹ ਸਭ ਮਿਲ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਆਪ ਟਾਈਪ ਕੀਤੇ ਕਰਲੀ ਬਰੇਸ { } ਸਾਦਾ ਟੈਕਸਟ ਹੀ ਰਹਿੰਦੇ ਹਨ, ਇਸ ਲਈ ਤੁਹਾਡੇ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਾਲੇ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਪਹਿਲਾਂ ਵਾਂਗ ਹੀ ਪੇਸਟ ਹੁੰਦੇ ਹਨ। /  / ਥਾਂ ਹੁਣ ਬਹੁਤ ਜ਼ਿਆਦਾ ਹੈ। ਪਹਿਲਾਂ 100 ਟੈਕਸਟ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਅਤੇ 25 ਤਸਵੀਰਾਂ ਰੱਖੀਆਂ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀਆਂ ਸਨ, ਹੁਣ 5,000 ਟੈਕਸਟ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਅਤੇ 500 ਤਸਵੀਰਾਂ ਰੱਖੀਆਂ ਜਾ ਸਕਦੀਆਂ ਹਨ। ਇੱਕ ਕਟਲਿੰਗ ਵਿੱਚ 10,000 ਅੱਖਰ ਤੱਕ ਆ ਜਾਂਦੇ ਹਨ, ਇੰਨੇ ਵਿੱਚ ਪੂਰੀ ਈਮੇਲ ਸਮਾ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ। | Write the reply just once; it comes in useful for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now keep a blank space for the part that changes, such as a name or an order number. When you pick such a cutling with ⌘⇧V, a short form asks for the name before pasting, and the names you used before are found in one click. /  / For dates you don't have to do anything. A cutling can keep today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and the right date or time comes out for the day you use the cutling. A cutling can also have you choose from a few options (such as the Basic or the Premium plan), or can bring in the text you copied last. While editing a cutling there is an ‘Add Variable’ button right below the text; click it and all of this is there. Curly braces { } you typed yourself stay plain text, so your earlier cutlings paste just as before. /  / There is now much more room. Before, 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be kept; now 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images can be kept. One cutling takes up to 10,000 characters; a whole email fits in that much. |

| pl | N_ios | Odpowiedź piszesz raz i pasuje do każdego, komu ją wysyłasz. W cutlingu możesz teraz zostawić puste pole na to, co za każdym razem jest inne, na przykład imię albo numer zamówienia. Gdy stukniesz taki cutling, klawiatura Cutling zapyta o imię, pokaże imiona użyte wcześniej, żeby wystarczyło jedno stuknięcie, a potem wpisze całą odpowiedź. Możesz też najpierw wpisać imię i dopiero wtedy stuknąć cutling. /  / O daty nie musisz się martwić. W cutlingu może być dzisiejsza data, jutrzejsza, data za tydzień albo aktualna godzina, a w dniu, w którym go użyjesz, wpiszą się poprawnie. Cutling może też dać do wyboru kilka opcji, na przykład plan „Podstawowy” albo „Premium”, wstawić ostatnio skopiowany tekst albo zostawić kursor tam, gdzie chcesz pisać dalej. Wszystko to znajdziesz pod przyciskiem „Zmienne” podczas edycji cutlinga. Nawiasy klamrowe wpisane ręcznie zostają zwykłym tekstem, więc cutlingi, które już masz, działają tak jak dotąd. /  / Cutling piszesz teraz tak, jak w aplikacji Notatki od Apple, więc nie musisz się niczego uczyć. Tekst cutlinga otwiera się jako osobna strona, a na niej masz pogrubienie, kursywę, podkreślenie, przekreślenie, zakreślacz, czcionkę monospace, linki oraz listy z punktorami, z myślnikami i numerowane. /  / Miejsca jest dużo więcej: 5000 cutlingów tekstowych i 500 obrazów zamiast 100 i 25. Jeden cutling może mieć do 10 000 znaków, a to wystarczy na cały e-mail. /  / Na klawiaturze Cutling pisze teraz każdy klawisz. Sformatowany cutling po stuknięciu wpisuje się jak każdy inny, a gdy go dotkniesz i przytrzymasz, kopiuje się razem z formatowaniem. /  / Jeśli do tej pory nie udało się dodać klawiatury Cutling do końca, otwórz w aplikacji Cutling ekran „Konfiguracja klawiatury”. Nowy przewodnik pokazuje trzy kroki w aplikacji Ustawienia na krótkim filmie. | You write a reply once and it fits everyone you send it to. In a cutling you can now leave an empty field for what is different each time, for example a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard will ask for the name, show the names used earlier so that one tap is enough, and then type the whole reply. You can also type the name first and only then tap the cutling. /  / You do not have to worry about dates. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's, the date a week from now or the current time, and on the day you use it they will be typed correctly. A cutling can also give several options to choose from, for example the plan "Basic" or "Premium", insert the last copied text, or leave the cursor where you want to keep writing. You will find all of this under the button "Variables" while editing a cutling. Curly braces typed by hand stay ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as they did until now. /  / You now write a cutling the way you do in Apple's Notes app, so you do not have to learn anything. A cutling's text opens as a separate page, and on it you have bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlighter, monospace font, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is much more room: 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images instead of 100 and 25. One cutling can have up to 10,000 characters, and that is enough for a whole email. /  / On the Cutling keyboard every key now types. A formatted cutling, when tapped, is typed like any other, and when you touch and hold it, it is copied together with its formatting. /  / If adding the Cutling keyboard has not been finished so far, open the "Keyboard Setup" screen in the Cutling app. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app in a short video. |

São 5000 cutlings de texto e 500 imagens, onde antes cabiam 100 e 25, e cada cutling pode ter até 10 000 caracteres, o que chega para um e-mail inteiro. | It is enough to write a reply one single time for it to serve all the people you send it to. A cutling can now have a blank space for the part that changes, such as the person's name or the order number. On picking that cutling with ⌘⇧V, a small form asks the name before pasting, and the names you have already used are one click away. /  / Dates give you no work at all: today's, tomorrow's, the one a week from now or the time come out right on the day the cutling is used. A cutling can also let you choose between options (Base plan or Premium plan, for example) or fetch the last text you copied. It is all in the “Add Variable” button, right below the text when editing a cutling. The curly braces written by you continue to be normal text, so the cutlings you already have are pasted as before. /  / And much more fits. That is 5000 text cutlings and 500 images, where before 100 and 25 fit, and each cutling can have up to 10 000 characters, which is enough for a whole email. |

| ro | N_ios | Scrii un răspuns o singură dată și e bun pentru toți cei cărora îl trimiți. Un cutling poate avea acum un spațiu gol pentru partea care se schimbă, de pildă un nume sau un număr de comandă. Când atingi un astfel de cutling, tastatura Cutling îți cere numele, îți arată numele folosite înainte, ca să alegi unul dintr-o atingere, apoi scrie răspunsul întreg. Poți și să scrii întâi numele și abia apoi să atingi cutling-ul. /  / Cu datele nu ai nimic de făcut. Un cutling poate conține data de azi, cea de mâine, cea de peste o săptămână sau ora, iar ce apare e corect în ziua în care îl folosești. Un cutling îți poate da și de ales între mai multe variante (de exemplu planul Standard sau Premium), poate aduce ultimul text copiat sau poate lăsa cursorul acolo unde vrei să scrii mai departe. Pe toate le găsești sub butonul „Variabile”, când editezi un cutling. Acoladele pe care le-ai tastat chiar tu rămân text obișnuit, așa că toate cutling-urile pe care le ai deja merg ca până acum. /  / Poți scrie un cutling așa cum scrii în aplicația Notițe de la Apple, fără să înveți nimic nou. Textul cutling-ului se deschide acum pe o pagină separată, cu aceste formate: aldin, cursiv, subliniat, text tăiat, evidențiere, lățime fixă, linkuri și liste cu buline, cu liniuțe sau numerotate. /  / Ai mult mai mult loc. Încap 5.000 de cutling-uri text și 500 de imagini, față de 100 și 25 până acum. Un cutling poate avea până la 10.000 de caractere, cât un e-mail întreg. /  / Pe tastatura Cutling, acum fiecare tastă scrie. Dacă atingi un cutling formatat, se scrie la fel ca oricare altul. Dacă îl ții apăsat, se copiază cu tot cu formatare. /  / Dacă n-ai apucat să termini de adăugat tastatura Cutling, deschide „Configurare tastatură” în Cutling. Noul ghid îți arată într-un videoclip scurt cei trei pași din aplicația Configurări. | You write a reply a single time and it is good for all those you send it to. A cutling can now have an empty space for the part that changes, for instance a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks you for the name, shows you the names used before, so that you choose one with a single tap, then writes the whole reply. You can also write the name first and only then tap the cutling. /  / With dates you have nothing to do. A cutling can contain today's date, tomorrow's, the one a week from now or the time, and what appears is correct on the day you use it. A cutling can also let you choose between several options (for example the Standard or Premium plan), can bring in the last copied text or can leave the cursor where you want to keep writing. You find all of them under the "Variabile" (Variables) button, when you edit a cutling. The curly braces you typed yourself remain ordinary text, so all the cutlings you already have work as until now. /  / You can write a cutling the way you write in the Notes app from Apple, without learning anything new. The cutling's text now opens on a separate page, with these formats: bold, italic, underlined, struck-through text, highlight, fixed width, links and lists with bullets, with dashes or numbered. /  / You have much more room. 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images fit, compared with 100 and 25 until now. A cutling can have up to 10,000 characters, as much as a whole email. /  / On the Cutling keyboard, every key now writes. If you tap a formatted cutling, it is written the same as any other. If you hold it pressed, it is copied together with its formatting. /  / If you did not get to finish adding the Cutling keyboard, open "Configurare tastatură" (Keyboard Setup) in Cutling. The new guide shows you in a short video the three steps in the Settings app. |

| ru | N_ios | Напишите ответ один раз, и он подойдёт каждому, кому вы его отправляете. В катлинге теперь можно оставить пропуск для того, что меняется, например для имени или номера заказа. Нажмите на такой катлинг, и клавиатура Cutling спросит имя, покажет те, что вы уже вводили, чтобы выбрать нужное одним нажатием, и напечатает ответ целиком. Ещё катлинг может предложить выбор, например между тарифами «Базовый» и «Премиум», подставить текст, который вы скопировали последним, или оставить курсор там, где вы хотите печатать дальше. Всё это есть под кнопкой «Переменные», когда вы редактируете катлинг. Фигурные скобки, которые вы напечатали сами, остаются обычным текстом, так что прежние катлинги работают как раньше. /  / Писать катлинг теперь можно так же, как в приложении «Заметки» от Apple, и учиться ничему новому не придётся. Текст катлинга открывается отдельной страницей, а на ней есть жирный шрифт, курсив, подчёркивание, зачёркивание, выделение цветом, моноширинный шрифт, ссылки и списки: маркированные, с тире и нумерованные. /  / Места стало намного больше. Теперь помещается 5000 текстовых катлингов и 500 картинок, а раньше было 100 и 25. В одном катлинге может быть до 10 000 символов, этого хватит на целое письмо. /  / На клавиатуре Cutling теперь печатает каждая клавиша. Нажмите на катлинг с форматированием, и он напечатается, как любой другой. Если нажать на него и удерживать, он скопируется вместе с форматированием. /  / Если вы так и не добавили клавиатуру Cutling, откройте в Cutling раздел «Настройка клавиатуры». Новое руководство показывает три шага в приложении «Настройки» коротким видео. | Write a reply one time, and it will suit everyone you send it to. Cutling ti lahko ponudi tudi izbiro med več možnostmi (recimo paket Osnovni ali Premium), prinese nazadnje kopirano besedilo ali pusti kazalec tam, kjer želiš pisati naprej. Vse to najdeš pod gumbom „Spremenljivke“, ko urejaš cutling. Zaviti oklepaji, vtipkani ročno, ostanejo navadno besedilo, zato cutlingi, ki jih že imaš, delujejo kot doslej. /  / Cutling lahko pišeš tako, kot pišeš v Applovi aplikaciji Zapiski (Notes), brez učenja česa novega. Besedilo cutlinga se zdaj odpre kot samostojna stran s temi možnostmi oblikovanja: krepko, poševno, podčrtano, prečrtano, označevanje, enakomerna širina, povezave ter seznami z oznakami, s pomišljaji in oštevilčeni seznami. /  / Prostora je veliko več. Shraniš lahko 5.000 besedilnih cutlingov in 500 slik, prej jih je bilo 100 in 25. En cutling ima lahko do 10.000 znakov, kar je dovolj za celo e-poštno sporočilo. /  / Na Cutling tipkovnici zdaj vsaka tipka vnaša. Ko tapneš oblikovan cutling, se vtipka kot vsak drug. Ko ga pridržiš, se kopira skupaj z oblikovanjem. /  / Če dodajanje Cutling tipkovnice še ni dokončano, v Cutlingu odpri „Nastavitev tipkovnice“. Novi vodič v kratkem videu pokaže tri korake v aplikaciji Nastavitve. | You write a reply once and it holds for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now have an empty place for the part that changes, for example a name or an order number. When you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks you for the name, shows the earlier names so that you pick one with a single tap, and then types in the whole reply. You can also type the name first and only then tap the cutling. /  / With dates you have no work at all. A cutling can contain today's date, tomorrow's, the date a week from now or the time of day, and in the text there is always written what holds on the day you use it. A cutling can also offer you a choice between several options (say the Basic or Premium package), bring in the text copied last or leave the cursor where you want to write on. You find all of this under the "Spremenljivke" (Variables) button when you edit a cutling. Curly braces typed in by hand remain ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as before. /  / You can write a cutling the way you write in Apple's app Zapiski (Notes), without learning anything new. The cutling's text now opens as a page of its own with these formatting options: bold, italic, underlined, struck through, highlighting, even width, links and lists with marks, with dashes and numbered lists. /  / There is much more room. You can save 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images; before there were 100 and 25. One cutling can have up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email message. /  / On the Cutling keyboard every key now types. When you tap a formatted cutling, it is typed in like any other. When you hold it, it is copied together with its formatting. /  / If adding the Cutling keyboard is not yet finished, open "Nastavitev tipkovnice" (Keyboard Setup) in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app in a short video. |
| sl-SI | N_mac | Odgovor napišeš enkrat in velja za vsakogar, ki mu ga pošlješ. Cutling ima zdaj lahko prazno mesto za del, ki se spreminja, na primer ime ali številko naročila. Ko tak cutling izbereš z ⌘⇧V, te kratek obrazec pred lepljenjem vpraša za ime, prejšnja imena pa so oddaljena en klik. /  / Z datumi nimaš nobenega dela: današnji datum, jutrišnji, datum čez teden dni ali ura so vedno taki, kot veljajo na dan, ko cutling uporabiš. Cutling ti lahko ponudi tudi izbiro med več možnostmi (recimo paket Osnovni ali Premium) ali prinese nazadnje kopirano besedilo. Vse to najdeš pod gumbom „Dodaj spremenljivko“, ki je tik pod besedilom, ko urejaš cutling. Zaviti oklepaji, vtipkani ročno, ostanejo navadno besedilo, zato se cutlingi, ki jih že imaš, lepijo kot doslej. /  / Prostora je veliko več: 5.000 besedilnih cutlingov in 500 slik, prej 100 in 25. En cutling ima lahko do 10.000 znakov, kar je dovolj za celo e-poštno sporočilo. | You write a reply once and it holds for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now have an empty place for the part that changes, for example a name or an order number. When you choose such a cutling with ⌘⇧V, a short form asks you for the name before pasting, and the earlier names are one click away. /  / With dates you have no work at all: today's date, tomorrow's, the date a week from now or the time of day are always as they hold on the day you use the cutling. A cutling can also offer you a choice between several options (say the Basic or Premium package) or bring in the text copied last. You find all of this under the "Dodaj spremenljivko" (Add Variable) button, which is right below the text when you edit a cutling. Curly braces typed in by hand remain ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have paste as before. /  / There is much more room: 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images, before 100 and 25. One cutling can have up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email message. |
| sr | N_ios | Напишите одговор једном и послужиће вам за свакога коме га шаљете. У катлингу сада можете да оставите празнину за део који се мења, на пример за име или број поруџбине. Додирните такав катлинг и Cutling тастатура ће вас питати за име, приказати имена која сте раније уносили, да једно изаберете једним додиром, а онда откуцати цео одговор. Може и брже: прво откуцајте име, па додирните катлинг. /  / За датуме не морате ништа да радите. У катлингу може да стоји данашњи датум, сутрашњи, датум за недељу дана или тренутно време, и биће тачни оног дана кад га употребите. Један катлинг може и да вам понуди избор, рецимо између пакета „Основни” и „Премијум”, да убаци последњи текст који сте копирали или да остави курсор тамо где желите да наставите да куцате. Све то је под дугметом „Променљиве” док уређујете катлинг. Витичасте заграде које сте сами откуцали остају обичан текст, па катлинзи које већ имате раде као и до сада. /  / Сада катлинг пишете онако како пишете у Apple-овој апликацији Notes (Белешке), па нема шта ново да учите. Текст катлинга отвара се као посебна страница, а на њој су подебљана слова, курзив, подвлачење, прецртавање, истицање бојом, слова једнаке ширине, линкови и листе: са тачкама, са цртицама и нумерисане. /  / Места има много више. Сада стаје 5.000 текстуалних катлинга и 500 слика, а до сада је стајало 100 и 25. Један катлинг може да има до 10.000 знакова, што је довољно за цео имејл. /  / На Cutling тастатури сада куца сваки тастер. Додирните форматиран катлинг и откуцаће се као и сваки други. Ако га додирнете и задржите, копираће се заједно са форматирањем. /  / Ако никада нисте до краја додали Cutling тастатуру, отворите „Подешавање тастатуре” у Cutling-у. Нови водич приказује три корака у апликацији „Подешавања” као кратак видео. | Write a reply once and it will serve you for everyone you send it to. In a cutling you can now leave a gap for the part that changes, for example for a name or an order number. Tap such a cutling and the Cutling keyboard will ask you for the name, show the names you entered before, so you pick one with one tap, and then type the whole reply. It can be quicker too: first type the name, then tap the cutling. /  / For dates you do not have to do anything. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's, the date a week from now or the current time, and they will be correct on the day you use it. A cutling can also offer you a choice, say between the plans "Basic" and "Premium", put in the last text you copied, or leave the cursor where you want to keep typing. All of that is under the "Variables" button while you edit a cutling. Curly braces you typed yourself remain ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as they have until now. /  / You now write a cutling the way you write in Apple's Notes app, so there is nothing new for you to learn. The cutling's text opens as a separate page, and on it are bold letters, italic, underlining, strikethrough, colour highlighting, letters of equal width, links and lists: with bullets, with dashes and numbered. /  / There is much more room. 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images now fit, and until now 100 and 25 fitted. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app as a short video. |

| sw | N_ios | Sasa unaandika jibu mara moja tu, nalo linawafaa wote unaowaandikia. Cutling inaweza kuwa na nafasi tupu kwa sehemu inayobadilika, kama jina au nambari ya oda. Unaweza pia kuandika jina kwanza, kisha ukagusa cutling. /  / Kuhusu tarehe, huna la kufanya. Yote haya yako kwenye kitufe cha “Vigezo” unapohariri cutling. Cutling moja inaweza kufika herufi 10,000, zinazotosha barua pepe nzima. /  / Kila kitufe kwenye kibodi ya Cutling sasa huandika. Ukigusa cutling yenye muundo, huandikwa kama nyingine yoyote. எழுதுவது போலவே கட்லிங்குவையும் எழுதலாம், புதிதாகக் கற்க எதுவும் இல்லை. கட்லிங்குவின் உரை இப்போது தனிப் பக்கமாகத் திறக்கும். அதில் தடித்த எழுத்து, சாய்வு எழுத்து, அடிக்கோடு, குறுக்குக்கோடு, ஹைலைட், மோனோஸ்பேஸ், இணைப்புகள், புள்ளியிட்ட, கோடிட்ட, எண்ணிட்ட பட்டியல்கள் ஆகியவை உள்ளன. /  / இடமும் பல மடங்கு கூடியிருக்கிறது. முன்பு 100 உரை கட்லிங்குகளும் 25 படங்களும்தான் சேமிக்க முடிந்தது. இப்போது 5,000 உரை கட்லிங்குகளும் 500 படங்களும் சேமிக்கலாம். ஒரு கட்லிங்குவில் 10,000 எழுத்துகள் வரை எழுதலாம், ஒரு முழு மின்னஞ்சலுக்கு அது போதும். /  / Cutling விசைப்பலகையில் உள்ள எல்லா விசைகளும் இப்போது தட்டச்சு செய்யும். வடிவமைத்த கட்லிங்குவைத் தட்டினால் மற்றவை போலவே தட்டச்சாகும். அதை அழுத்திப் பிடித்தால் வடிவமைப்புடன் நகலாகும். /  / Cutling விசைப்பலகையை இன்னும் சேர்த்து முடிக்கவில்லை என்றால், Cutling-இல் “விசைப்பலகை அமைப்பு” என்பதைத் திறங்கள். அமைப்புகள் ஆப்பில் செய்ய வேண்டிய மூன்று படிகளையும் புதிய வழிகாட்டி ஒரு சிறு வீடியோவாகக் காட்டும். | Writing a reply once is enough; whoever you send it to, that same one serves. For the part that changes each time, for example a customer's name or an order number, you can now leave a blank in a cutling. As soon as you tap that cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name. It also shows the names you used before, and you can pick one of them with a single tap. Then it types the whole reply itself. If you want it faster still, you can type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For the date you need do nothing. In a cutling you can put today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use it, it comes out right. In a cutling you can put a few choices and pick one of them (for example the Basic or Premium plan), bring in the text you copied last, or stop the cursor at the place where you want to go on typing. While editing a cutling, all of these are in the “Variables” button. Curly brackets you typed yourself stay as ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as before. /  / You can write a cutling just as you write in Apple's Notes app; there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens as a page of its own. It has bold lettering, italic lettering, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / Room, too, has grown many times over. Before, only 100 text cutlings and 25 images could be saved. Now you can save 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images. In one cutling you can write up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email. /  / All the keys on the Cutling keyboard now type. If you tap a formatted cutling it gets typed like the others. If you touch and hold it, it is copied with its formatting. /  / If you have not yet finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open “Keyboard Setup” in Cutling. The new guide shows all three steps to do in the Settings app as a short video. |

| te-IN | N_ios | జవాబు ఒక్కసారి రాస్తే చాలు, ఎవరికి పంపినా అదే పనికొస్తుంది. ప్రతిసారీ మారే భాగానికి, అంటే కస్టమర్ పేరు లేదా ఆర్డర్ నంబర్ లాంటి వాటికి, ఇప్పుడు కట్లింగ్‌లో ఒక ఖాళీ వదలవచ్చు. ఆ కట్లింగ్‌ను నొక్కగానే Cutling కీబోర్డ్ పేరు అడుగుతుంది. ఇంతకుముందు వాడిన పేర్లను కూడా చూపిస్తుంది, వాటిలో ఒకదాన్ని ఒక్క నొక్కుతో ఎంచుకోవచ్చు. ఆ తర్వాత పూర్తి జవాబును అదే టైప్ చేస్తుంది. ఇంకా త్వరగా కావాలంటే ముందు పేరు టైప్ చేసి, ఆపై కట్లింగ్‌ను నొక్కండి. /  / తేదీల కోసం మీరు ఏమీ చేయనక్కర్లేదు. కట్లింగ్‌లో ఈరోజు తేదీ, రేపటి తేదీ, వారం తర్వాతి తేదీ లేదా సమయం పెట్టుకోవచ్చు, వాడే రోజున అది సరిగ్గా వస్తుంది. కట్లింగ్‌లో కొన్ని ఎంపికలు పెట్టి వాటిలో ఒకటి ఎంచుకోవచ్చు (ఉదాహరణకు బేసిక్ లేదా ప్రీమియం ప్లాన్), చివరిగా కాపీ చేసిన టెక్స్ట్‌ను అందులోకి తెచ్చుకోవచ్చు, లేదా మీరు టైప్ చేయడం కొనసాగించాలనుకునే చోట కర్సర్‌ను ఆపవచ్చు. కట్లింగ్‌ను సవరించేటప్పుడు ఇవన్నీ “వేరియబుల్స్” బటన్‌లో ఉంటాయి. మీరే టైప్ చేసిన కర్లీ బ్రేస్‌లు మామూలు టెక్స్ట్‌గానే ఉంటాయి, కాబట్టి మీ దగ్గర ఇప్పటికే ఉన్న కట్లింగ్‌లు ఇంతకుముందులాగే పనిచేస్తాయి. /  / Apple నోట్స్ యాప్‌లో రాసినట్టే కట్లింగ్‌ను రాయవచ్చు, కొత్తగా నేర్చుకోవాల్సింది ఏమీ లేదు. కట్లింగ్ టెక్స్ట్ ఇప్పుడు ప్రత్యేక పేజీలో తెరుచుకుంటుంది. అందులో బోల్డ్, ఇటాలిక్, అండర్‌లైన్, స్ట్రైక్‌త్రూ, హైలైట్, మోనోస్పేస్, లింక్‌లు, బుల్లెట్, డాష్, సంఖ్యల జాబితాలు ఉన్నాయి. /  / చోటు కూడా చాలా పెరిగింది. ఇంతకుముందు 100 వచనం కట్లింగ్‌లు, 25 చిత్రాలే సేవ్ చేయగలిగేవారు. ఇప్పుడు 5,000 వచనం కట్లింగ్‌లు, 500 చిత్రాలు సేవ్ చేయవచ్చు. ఒక్క కట్లింగ్‌లో 10,000 అక్షరాల వరకు రాయవచ్చు, ఒక పూర్తి ఈమెయిల్‌కు అది సరిపోతుంది. /  / Cutling కీబోర్డ్‌లో ఇప్పుడు ప్రతి కీ టైప్ అవుతుంది. ఫార్మాట్ చేసిన కట్లింగ్‌ను నొక్కితే మిగతావాటిలాగే టైప్ అవుతుంది. దాన్ని నొక్కి పట్టుకుంటే ఫార్మాటింగ్‌తో సహా కాపీ అవుతుంది. /  / Cutling కీబోర్డ్‌ను జోడించడం ఇంకా పూర్తి చేయకపోతే, Cutlingలో “కీబోర్డ్ సెటప్” తెరవండి. సెట్టింగ్స్ యాప్‌లో చేయాల్సిన మూడు దశలను కొత్త గైడ్ చిన్న వీడియోలో చూపిస్తుంది. | Writing a reply once is enough; whoever you send it to, the same one does the job. For the part that changes each time, meaning things like a customer's name or an order number, you can now leave a blank in a cutling. As soon as you tap that cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name. It also shows the names you used before, and you can pick one of them with a single tap. After that it types the whole reply itself. If you want it quicker still, type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For dates you need do nothing. In a cutling you can put today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use it, it comes out right. In a cutling you can put a few choices and pick one of them (for example the Basic or Premium plan), bring in the text you copied last, or stop the cursor at the place where you want to go on typing. While editing a cutling, all of these are in the “Variables” button. Curly braces you typed yourself stay as ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as before. /  / You can write a cutling just as you write in the Apple Notes app; there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens on a page of its own. It has bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / Room has grown a lot too. Before, you could save only 100 text cutlings and 25 images. Now you can save 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images. In a single cutling you can write up to 10,000 characters, which is enough for a whole email. /  / On the Cutling keyboard every key now gets typed. If you tap a formatted cutling it gets typed like the others. If you touch and hold it, it is copied along with its formatting. /  / If you have not yet finished adding the Cutling keyboard, open “Keyboard Setup” in Cutling. The new guide shows the three steps to do in the Settings app in a short video. |

| th | N_ios | เขียนคำตอบครั้งเดียว แล้วใช้ได้กับทุกคนที่คุณส่งให้ ตอนนี้คัตลิงหนึ่งรายการมีช่องว่างสำหรับส่วนที่เปลี่ยนไปในแต่ละครั้งได้ เช่น ชื่อ หรือหมายเลขคำสั่งซื้อ เมื่อคุณแตะคัตลิงนั้น คีย์บอร์ด Cutling จะถามชื่อ แสดงชื่อที่คุณเคยใช้ให้แตะเลือกได้ในครั้งเดียว แล้วพิมพ์คำตอบให้ครบทั้งข้อความ หรือจะพิมพ์ชื่อก่อนแล้วค่อยแตะคัตลิงก็ได้ /  / เรื่องวันที่ คุณไม่ต้องทำอะไรเลย ในคัตลิงใส่วันที่ของวันนี้ พรุ่งนี้ อีก 1 สัปดาห์ข้างหน้า หรือเวลาได้ และจะออกมาถูกต้องตามวันที่คุณใช้ ในคัตลิงยังใส่ตัวเลือกให้เลือกได้ด้วย เช่น แพ็กเกจรายเดือนหรือแพ็กเกจรายปี ดึงข้อความที่คุณคัดลอกล่าสุดมาใส่ได้ หรือให้เคอร์เซอร์ไปอยู่ตรงจุดที่คุณอยากพิมพ์ต่อก็ได้ ทั้งหมดนี้อยู่ใต้ปุ่ม “ตัวแปร” ตอนแก้ไขคัตลิง ส่วนวงเล็บปีกกาที่คุณพิมพ์เองยังเป็นข้อความธรรมดา คัตลิงที่คุณมีอยู่แล้วจึงทำงานเหมือนเดิม /  / คุณเขียนคัตลิงได้แบบเดียวกับที่เขียนในแอปโน้ตของ Apple ไม่ต้องเรียนรู้อะไรใหม่ ข้อความของคัตลิงเปิดเป็นหน้าของตัวเองแล้ว และจัดรูปแบบได้ทั้งตัวหนา ตัวเอียง ขีดเส้นใต้ ขีดทับ ไฮไลต์ โมโนสเปซ ลิงก์ รวมถึงรายการแบบจุด แบบขีด และแบบตัวเลข /  / มีพื้นที่มากกว่าเดิมมาก เก็บคัตลิงข้อความได้ 5,000 รายการและภาพ 500 ภาพ จากเดิม 100 และ 25 คัตลิงหนึ่งรายการยาวได้ถึง 10,000 อักขระ พอสำหรับอีเมลทั้งฉบับ /  / ทุกปุ่มบนคีย์บอร์ด Cutling พิมพ์ได้แล้ว แตะคัตลิงที่จัดรูปแบบไว้ก็พิมพ์ได้เหมือนคัตลิงอื่น ส่วนการกดค้างจะคัดลอกคัตลิงนั้นไปพร้อมรูปแบบ /  / หากคุณยังเพิ่มคีย์บอร์ด Cutling ไม่เสร็จ ให้เปิด “การตั้งค่าคีย์บอร์ด” ใน Cutling คู่มือใหม่แสดงสามขั้นตอนในแอปการตั้งค่าเป็นวิดีโอสั้น ๆ | Write a reply once, and it works for everyone you send it to. Biçimli bir cutling'e dokunduğunda o da diğerleri gibi yazılır. Basılı tutarsan biçimiyle birlikte kopyalanır. /  / Cutling klavyesini eklemeyi yarıda bıraktıysan Cutling'de “Klavye kurulumu”nu aç. In a cutling you can now leave a blank for the part that changes, for example for a name or an order number. If you like, you can also type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / You need to do nothing for dates. A cutling can contain today's date, tomorrow's date, the date of a week later or the time; the correct one is typed on the day you use it. A cutling can also have you make a choice among options (for example the "Standard" or "Premium" plan), bring the text you copied last, or leave the cursor at the place where you want to go on typing. When you tap a formatted cutling, it too is typed like the others. The new guide shows the three steps in the Settings app with a short video. |

| uk | N_ios | Напишіть відповідь один раз, і вона підійде кожному, кому ви її надсилаєте. У катлінгу тепер можна залишити пропуск для того, що змінюється, наприклад для імені чи номера замовлення. Натисніть на такий катлінг, і клавіатура Cutling спитає ім’я, покаже ті, що ви вже вводили, щоб вибрати потрібне одним натисканням, і надрукує всю відповідь. Можна й швидше: спершу надрукувати ім’я, а потім натиснути на катлінг. /  / З датами нічого робити не треба. У катлінгу може стояти сьогоднішня дата, завтрашня, дата через тиждень або поточний час, і в день, коли ви ним користуєтеся, вони будуть правильні. Ще катлінг може запропонувати вибір, наприклад між тарифами «Базовий» і «Преміум», підставити текст, який ви скопіювали останнім, або залишити курсор там, де ви хочете друкувати далі. Усе це є під кнопкою «Змінні», коли ви редагуєте катлінг. Фігурні дужки, які ви надрукували самі, залишаються звичайним текстом, тож давніші катлінги працюють як раніше. /  / Писати катлінг тепер можна так само, як у застосунку «Нотатки» від Apple, і нічого нового вчити не доведеться. Текст катлінга відкривається окремою сторінкою, а на ній є жирний шрифт, курсив, підкреслення, закреслення, виділення кольором, моноширинний шрифт, посилання та списки: з маркерами, з тире й нумеровані. /  / Місця стало набагато більше. Тепер уміщається 5000 текстових катлінгів і 500 зображень, а раніше було 100 і 25. В одному катлінгу може бути до 10 000 символів, цього вистачить на цілий лист. /  / На клавіатурі Cutling тепер друкує кожна клавіша. Натисніть на форматований катлінг, і він надрукується, як будь-який інший. Якщо натиснути на нього й утримувати, він скопіюється разом із форматуванням. /  / Якщо ви так і не додали клавіатуру Cutling, відкрийте в Cutling розділ «Налаштування клавіатури». Новий посібник показує три кроки в застосунку «Налаштування» коротким відео. | Write a reply one time, and it will suit everyone you send it to. In a cutling you can now leave a gap for what changes, for example for a name or an order number. Tap such a cutling, and the Cutling keyboard will ask for the name, show the ones you have already entered so you can pick the right one with one tap, and type the whole reply. It can be quicker too: first type the name, and then tap the cutling. /  / With dates there is nothing you have to do. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's, the date a week from now or the current time, and on the day you use it they will be correct. A cutling can also offer a choice, for example between the plans "Basic" and "Premium", put in the text you copied last, or leave the cursor where you want to keep typing. All of this is under the "Variables" button when you edit a cutling. | ur-PK | N_ios | جواب ایک بار لکھیں، پھر جسے بھی بھیجیں، وہی کام آئے گا۔ جو حصہ ہر بار بدلتا ہے، جیسے نام یا آرڈر نمبر، اُس کے لیے اب کٹلنگ میں خالی جگہ رکھی جا سکتی ہے۔ ایسے کٹلنگ پر ٹیپ کرتے ہی Cutling کی بورڈ نام پوچھتا ہے، پہلے استعمال کیے ہوئے نام دکھاتا ہے تاکہ آپ ایک ٹیپ سے چن لیں، اور پھر پورا جواب ٹائپ کر دیتا ہے۔ چاہیں تو پہلے نام ٹائپ کریں اور پھر کٹلنگ پر ٹیپ کریں۔ /  / تاریخ کے لیے آپ کو کچھ نہیں کرنا پڑتا۔ کٹلنگ میں آج کی تاریخ، اگلے دن کی تاریخ، ایک ہفتے بعد کی تاریخ یا وقت رکھا جا سکتا ہے، اور جس دن آپ اسے استعمال کریں اُس دن وہ درست نکلتا ہے۔ کٹلنگ آپ کو چند آپشنز میں سے ایک چننے بھی دے سکتا ہے (جیسے بیسک یا پریمیم پلان)، آپ کا آخری بار کاپی کیا ہوا متن لا سکتا ہے، یا کرسر وہیں چھوڑ سکتا ہے جہاں سے آپ آگے ٹائپ کرنا چاہتے ہیں۔ کٹلنگ میں ترمیم کرتے وقت یہ سب «ویری ایبلز» بٹن میں ملتا ہے۔ جو قوسین { } آپ نے خود ٹائپ کی ہیں وہ عام متن ہی رہتی ہیں، اس لیے آپ کے پرانے کٹلنگ پہلے کی طرح ہی چلتے ہیں۔ /  / کٹلنگ اب ویسے ہی لکھیں جیسے ایپل کی Notes ایپ میں لکھتے ہیں؛ کچھ نیا سیکھنا نہیں پڑتا۔ کٹلنگ کا متن اب اپنے الگ صفحے پر کھلتا ہے، جہاں بولڈ، اِٹیلک، انڈر لائن، اسٹرائیک تھرو، ہائی لائٹ، مونو اسپیس، لنک، اور بلٹ، ڈیش اور نمبر والی فہرستیں ہیں۔ /  / گنجائش بھی بہت بڑھ گئی ہے۔ اب 5,000 متن کٹلنگ اور 500 تصویریں رکھی جا سکتی ہیں، جبکہ پہلے حد 100 اور 25 تھی۔ ایک کٹلنگ 10,000 حروف تک کا ہو سکتا ہے، یعنی پوری ای میل آ جاتی ہے۔ /  / Cutling کی بورڈ کی ہر کلید اب ٹائپ کرتی ہے۔ فارمیٹ والے کٹلنگ پر ٹیپ کریں تو وہ باقی کٹلنگ کی طرح ٹائپ ہو جاتا ہے۔ اسے دبائے رکھیں تو وہ اپنی فارمیٹنگ سمیت کاپی ہوتا ہے۔ /  / اگر آپ نے Cutling کی بورڈ شامل کرنا ادھورا چھوڑ دیا تھا تو Cutling میں «کیبورڈ سیٹ اپ» کھولیں۔ نئی گائیڈ ترتیبات ایپ کے تینوں مراحل ایک مختصر ویڈیو میں دکھاتی ہے۔ | Write a reply once; then whoever you send it to, that same one will do. For the part that changes every time, such as a name or an order number, a blank can now be kept in a cutling. As soon as you tap such a cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks the name, shows the names used before so that you pick one with a single tap, and then types the whole reply. If you like, type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / For the date you do not have to do anything. A cutling can hold today's date, the next day's date, the date a week later or the time, and on the day you use it, it comes out correct. A cutling can also let you choose one of a few options (such as the Basic or Premium plan), can bring the text you copied last, or can leave the cursor right where you want to go on typing. While editing a cutling, all of this is found in the "Variables" button. The braces { } you typed yourself stay ordinary text, so your old cutlings work just as before. /  / Now write a cutling the same way you write in Apple's Notes app; there is nothing new to learn. A cutling's text now opens on a separate page of its own, where there are bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / Room has also grown a lot. Now 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images can be kept, whereas the limit used to be 100 and 25. One cutling can be up to 10,000 characters, meaning a whole email fits. /  / Every key of the Cutling keyboard now types. Tap a cutling that has formatting and it gets typed like the other cutlings. Keep it pressed and it is copied together with its formatting. /  / If you had left adding the Cutling keyboard unfinished, open "Keyboard Setup" in Cutling. The new guide shows all three steps of the Settings app in a short video. |

| vi | N_ios | Câu trả lời chỉ cần viết một lần là dùng được cho mọi người bạn gửi. Giờ đây một cutling có thể có ô trống cho phần thay đổi, chẳng hạn tên hoặc mã đơn hàng. Khi bạn chạm vào cutling đó, bàn phím Cutling hỏi tên, hiện những tên bạn đã dùng trước đây để bạn chạm một lần là chọn xong, rồi gõ trọn câu trả lời. Bạn cũng có thể gõ tên trước rồi mới chạm vào cutling. /  / Ngày tháng thì bạn không phải làm gì. Một cutling có thể chứa ngày hôm nay, ngày mai, ngày này tuần sau hoặc giờ hiện tại, và luôn ra đúng vào hôm bạn dùng. Mỗi cutling còn có thể cho bạn chọn giữa vài lựa chọn (ví dụ Gói Cơ bản hoặc Gói Nâng cao), lấy đoạn văn bản bạn sao chép gần nhất, hoặc để con trỏ ở chỗ bạn muốn gõ tiếp. Tất cả nằm trong nút “Biến” khi bạn sửa một cutling. Dấu ngoặc nhọn bạn tự gõ vẫn là văn bản bình thường, nên những cutling bạn đã có vẫn hoạt động như trước. /  / Bạn viết cutling giống như viết trong ứng dụng Ghi chú của Apple, không phải học thêm gì. Văn bản của cutling giờ mở thành một trang riêng, với các định dạng đậm, nghiêng, gạch chân, gạch ngang, tô sáng, đơn cách, liên kết, cùng danh sách dấu đầu dòng, gạch đầu dòng và đánh số. /  / Chỗ chứa rộng hơn hẳn: 5.000 cutling văn bản và 500 hình ảnh, trước đây là 100 và 25. Một cutling dài được tới 10.000 ký tự, đủ cho cả một email. /  / Giờ phím nào trên bàn phím Cutling cũng gõ được. Chạm vào một cutling có định dạng thì nó được gõ ra như mọi cutling khác. Chạm giữ thì cutling đó được sao chép kèm nguyên định dạng. /  / Nếu bạn chưa thêm xong bàn phím Cutling, hãy mở “Cài đặt bàn phím” trong Cutling. Hướng dẫn mới chiếu ba bước trong ứng dụng Cài đặt bằng một video ngắn. | A reply only needs writing once and it can be used for everyone you send it to. A cutling can now have a blank box for the part that changes, such as a name or an order number. When you tap that cutling, the Cutling keyboard asks for the name, shows the names you have used before so that one tap picks one, then types the whole reply. You can also type the name first and only then tap the cutling. /  / As for dates, you do not have to do anything. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's, this day next week or the current time, and it always comes out right on the day you use it. Each cutling can also let you choose between a few options (for example Gói Cơ bản or Gói Nâng cao), bring in the text you copied most recently, or leave the cursor where you want to go on typing. All of it is in the “Biến” button when you edit a cutling. A cutling's text now opens as a page of its own, with the formats bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospace, links, and bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / The room to hold things is far bigger: 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images, where before it was 100 and 25. One cutling can be as long as 10,000 characters, enough for a whole email. /  / Now every key on the Cutling keyboard types. The new guide plays the three steps in the Settings app as a short video. |

| zh-Hans | N_ios | 回复写一次，发给谁都能用。现在剪切片段里可以留填空，把每次不一样的部分空出来，比如对方的名字、订单号。轻点这样的剪切片段，Cutling 键盘会先问你名字，还会列出你以前用过的名字，轻点一下就能选，然后输入完整的回复。你也可以先打出名字，再轻点剪切片段。 /  / 日期不用你管。剪切片段里可以放今天的日期、明天的日期、一周后的日期或当前时间，到用的那天，出来的都是对的。它还能让你在几个选项里挑一个，比如“标准版”和“专业版”，也能带入你最近一次复制的文字，或者把光标停在你想接着打字的地方。编辑剪切片段时，这些都在“变量”按钮里。你自己打的花括号还是普通文字，所以已有的剪切片段用起来和以前一样。 /  / 写剪切片段就像在 Apple 的“备忘录”里写东西一样，不用另外学。剪切片段的文字现在会单独打开一页，可以用粗体、斜体、下划线、删除线、高亮、等宽字体和链接，也可以用项目符号列表、短划线列表和编号列表。 /  / 容量大了很多。文本剪切片段能存 5,000 条，图片能存 500 张，以前是 100 条和 25 张。一条剪切片段最多可以有 10,000 个字符，放一整封邮件也够。 /  / Cutling 键盘上的每个键现在都能输入。带格式的剪切片段，轻点后和其他的一样直接输入，按住则连格式一起复制。 /  / 如果你一直没把 Cutling 键盘添加完，在 Cutling 里打开“键盘设置”就行。新的指南用一段短视频演示“设置”App 里的三个步骤。 | Write a reply once and it works for whoever you send it to. A cutling can now have a blank left in it, leaving empty the part that differs each time, such as the other person's name or an order number. Tap such a cutling and the Cutling keyboard first asks you for the name, and also lists the names you used before so one tap picks one, then types the complete reply. You can also type the name first and then tap the cutling. /  / Dates need nothing from you. A cutling can hold today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the current time, and on the day you use it what comes out is right. It can also let you pick one of several options, such as 'Standard' and 'Pro', bring in the text you copied most recently, or leave the cursor where you want to go on typing. While you edit a cutling, all of these are in the 'Variables' button. Curly braces you typed yourself are still ordinary text, so the cutlings you already have work as they did before. /  / Writing a cutling is just like writing in Apple's Notes, with nothing extra to learn. A cutling's text now opens as a page of its own, where you can use bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, highlight, monospaced type and links, and also bulleted, dashed and numbered lists. /  / There is far more room. You can keep 5,000 text cutlings and 500 images, where it used to be 100 and 25. One cutling can be up to 10,000 characters, enough even for a whole email. /  / Every key on the Cutling keyboard now types. A cutling with formatting types straight in like the others when tapped, and touching and holding it copies it together with its formatting. /  / If you never finished adding the Cutling keyboard, just open 'Keyboard Setup' in Cutling. Today's date, tomorrow's date, the date a week from now or the current time come out right on the day you use the cutling.
